000179: function formatTime(seconds) {
000180:   const safe = Math.max(0, toNumber(seconds, 0))
000181:   const mins = String(Math.floor(safe / 60)).padStart(2, '0')
000182:   const secs = String(Math.floor(safe % 60)).padStart(2, '0')
000183:   const ms = String(Math.floor((safe - Math.floor(safe)) * 1000)).padStart(3, '0')
000184:   return `${mins}:${secs}.${ms}`
000185: }

007074: def _mmaudio_append_unique(base: str, extra: str) -> str:
007075:     base_text = str(base or "").strip()
007076:     extra_text = str(extra or "").strip()
007077:     if not extra_text:
007078:         return base_text
007079:     lowered = base_text.lower()
007080:     parts = []
007081:     for chunk in [item.strip() for item in extra_text.split(",") if item.strip()]:
007082:         if chunk.lower() not in lowered:
007083:             parts.append(chunk)
007084:     if not parts:
007085:         return base_text
007086:     return (base_text + ", " if base_text else "") + ", ".join(parts)

008455: def _assembly_scene_audio_volume_for_item(item: dict[str, Any], audio_mode: str, scene_volume: float) -> float:
008456:     """AVA_BOARD_ASSEMBLY_SCENE_AUDIO_ABSOLUTE_V200U.
008457: 
008458:     Scene videos can contain generated dialogue/SFX. Earlier logic muted IA2V/lip-sync
008459:     scene audio whenever the selected mode referenced original/master audio. If the
008460:     master audio path is missing after restore/import, that makes the final Assembly
008461:     silent even though scene MP4 files have audio. Keep scene audio unless the user
008462:     explicitly selected original_only or the scene explicitly asks to mute it.
008463:     """
008464:     mode = str(audio_mode or "").lower()
008465:     data = item or {}
008466: 
008467:     if mode in {"original_only", "original_plus_music"}:
008468:         return 0.0
008469: 
008470:     explicit_mute = _assembly_bool(
008471:         data.get("mute_scene_audio")
008472:         or data.get("muteSceneAudio")
008473:         or data.get("scene_audio_muted")
008474:         or data.get("sceneAudioMuted")
008475:     )
008476:     if explicit_mute:
008477:         return 0.0
008478: 
008479:     return max(0.0, float(scene_volume))

008981:         db = store.get_db()

008984:         return {}

008987:     data = snap.get("data") if isinstance(snap, dict) else None
008988:     if not isinstance(data, dict):
008989:         print("[ASSEMBLY MMA AUDIO INDEX V212K]", {"project_id": pid, "count": 0, "reason": "no_audio_studio_snapshot"}, flush=True)
008990:         return {}

008992:     result: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
008993: 
008994:     def _txt(*values: Any) -> str:
008995:         for value in values:
008996:             if value is None:
008997:                 continue
008998:             text = str(value).strip()
008999:             if text:
009000:                 return text
009001:         return ""
009002: 
009003:     def _variant(scene: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
009004:         variants = scene.get("variants") if isinstance(scene.get("variants"), list) else []
009005:         wanted = _txt(
009006:             scene.get("audio_studio_applied_variant_id"),
009007:             scene.get("audioStudioAppliedVariantId"),
009008:             scene.get("appliedVariantId"),
009009:             scene.get("applied_variant_id"),
009010:             scene.get("selectedVariantId"),
009011:             scene.get("selected_variant_id"),
009012:         )
009013:         if wanted:
009014:             for item in variants:
009015:                 if isinstance(item, dict) and _txt(item.get("id"), item.get("variantId"), item.get("variant_id")) == wanted:
009016:                     return item
009017:         for item in variants:
009018:             if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("applied") is True:
009019:                 return item
009020:         return {}
009021: 
009022:     for scene in scenes:
009023:         if not isinstance(scene, dict):
009024:             continue
009025:         scene_id = _txt(scene.get("scene_id"), scene.get("sceneId"), scene.get("id"))
009026:         if not scene_id:
009027:             continue
009028:         variant = _variant(scene)
009029:         variant_id = _txt(
009030:             scene.get("audio_studio_applied_variant_id"),
009031:             scene.get("audioStudioAppliedVariantId"),

009036:             variant.get("variant_id") if isinstance(variant, dict) else "",
009037:         )
009038:         variant_id_lower = variant_id.lower()
009039:         if variant_id_lower.startswith("board_source_"):
009040:             continue
009041: 
009042:         status_text = " ".join(_txt(scene.get(key)) for key in (
009043:             "mmaudio_status", "mmaudioStatus", "audio_studio_status", "audioStudioStatus", "status"
009044:         )).lower()
009045:         prompt = _txt(
009046:             scene.get("mmaudio_prompt"),
009047:             scene.get("mmaudioPrompt"),
009048:             variant.get("prompt") if isinstance(variant, dict) else "",
009049:         )
009050:         is_real_mma = variant_id_lower.startswith("mmaudio_") or (prompt and ("applied" in status_text or "mmaudio" in status_text))
009051:         if not is_real_mma:
009052:             continue
009053: 
009054:         applied = scene.get("mmaudioAppliedVideo") or scene.get("mmaudio_applied_video") or scene.get("currentVideo") or scene.get("current_video") or {}
009055:         if not isinstance(applied, dict):
009056:             applied = {}
009057:         applied_variant_video = {}
009058:         if isinstance(variant, dict):
009059:             av = variant.get("appliedVideo") or variant.get("applied_video") or {}

006026: def _mmaudio_append_unique(base: str, extra: str) -> str:
006027:     base_text = str(base or "").strip()
006028:     extra_text = str(extra or "").strip()
006029:     if not extra_text:
006030:         return base_text
006031:     lowered = base_text.lower()
006032:     parts = []
006033:     for chunk in [item.strip() for item in extra_text.split(",") if item.strip()]:
006034:         if chunk.lower() not in lowered:
006035:             parts.append(chunk)
006036:     if not parts:
006037:         return base_text
006038:     return (base_text + ", " if base_text else "") + ", ".join(parts)

007408: def _assembly_scene_audio_volume_for_item(item: dict[str, Any], audio_mode: str, scene_volume: float) -> float:
007409:     """AVA_BOARD_ASSEMBLY_SCENE_AUDIO_ABSOLUTE_V200U.
007410: 
007411:     Scene videos can contain generated dialogue/SFX. Earlier logic muted IA2V/lip-sync
007412:     scene audio whenever the selected mode referenced original/master audio. If the
007413:     master audio path is missing after restore/import, that makes the final Assembly
007414:     silent even though scene MP4 files have audio. Keep scene audio unless the user
007415:     explicitly selected original_only or the scene explicitly asks to mute it.
007416:     """
007417:     mode = str(audio_mode or "").lower()
007418:     data = item or {}
007419: 
007420:     if mode in {"original_only", "original_plus_music"}:
007421:         return 0.0
007422: 
007423:     explicit_mute = _assembly_bool(
007424:         data.get("mute_scene_audio")
007425:         or data.get("muteSceneAudio")
007426:         or data.get("scene_audio_muted")
007427:         or data.get("sceneAudioMuted")
007428:     )
007429:     if explicit_mute:
007430:         return 0.0
007431: 
007432:     return max(0.0, float(scene_volume))

001583:     body: JSON.stringify({
001584:       data: snapshot,

001587:     }),
001588:   })

001590:     const text = await response.text().catch(() => '')
001591:     throw new Error(text || `board_assembly snapshot save failed ${response.status}`)
001592:   }
001593:   return response.json().catch(() => null)
001594: }

001596: function generatorAssetIdFromRef(...values) {
001597:   for (const value of values) {
001598:     const raw = String(value || '').trim()
001599:     if (!raw) continue
001600:     if (raw.startsWith('asset_')) return raw
001601:     const match = raw.match(/\/(?:api\/)?assets\/([^/]+)\/file/i)
001602:     if (match?.[1]) return decodeURIComponent(match[1])
001603:   }
001604:   return ''
001605: }
